CLEANED UP — NO KPI BAND, THE LEVERS AS A MATRIX, AND A RECORD KEPT BY HAND (Ray, 6 Oct 2026, a red cross over the six-tile band: "clean up stock section alittle bit, i was thining the lst should be table/ matrix for overview review (something like this blue screenshot)" — the coverage matrix — then "maybe there should be a manual table as well to keep record of it"): THE KPI BAND IS GONE — every figure it carried already stood on a card below it (the ad-spend book is the in-stock card's book tile, Google's in-stock share its Google tile, the rules + hero sizes the coverage matrix and the hero-size runs, the cut-offs and the findings their own cards), so the page opens on its first card; forbidden markers keep `id="kpis"` and `renderKpis` out. THE LEVERS' MARKET LIST IS A MATRIX in the coverage matrix's own shape, ABOVE the plan (overview first): the market named once with its products under it, a lever per column of one width with THE PLAN IN THE COLUMN HEAD ("BAU 35% · SALE not set"), ONE SHORT VALUE A CELL (≈36% measured · 21% from a rule name · runs · N/A · on · kept live · paused · —) — blue where a rule runs it on plan, orange WITH A DOT off plan (never colour alone), muted where no rule runs it — an Off plan count per market (✓ none), and the detail in the cell's tooltip (what runs, why, the target it is held to and whose — its own or the brand's, BAU or SALE — why it is off plan, its rules in run order, the latest record); the plan tiles below are headed Brand plan; a market's own values open in a row UNDER it (✎ toggles it) with Keep as it runs / ↺ Brand plan in that row. THE RECORD, KEPT BY HAND (KV stocklevers `r:<Brand>|<id>` {d, mk, k, mode, v, was?, note, src?, by, at, ed?}): the matrix says what the rules read, the record what a person SET in FeedHero — day, markets, lever, BAU/SALE, from → to, note, who — newest first (eight, then Show all), ＋ Add / ✎ / 🗑 / ⬇ CSV; ＋ Record it on a switch marked made writes its changes in one click, FROZEN the day written (engine switchRecords — a later plan edit never rewrites them), and the step then reads ✓ recorded; the latest record per market × lever rides that cell's tooltip and ⧉ Copy summary ends with the newest five; it never moves a reading. The worker checks every field (a real day, a lever, a value of that lever's kind, the brand's markets) and WHO RECORDED IT IS THE SERVER'S WORD, KEPT THROUGH EVERY EDIT (the editor stamped apart as ed — an edit can never rewrite who said so); leverView passes r: keys in scope. FOUND ON THE WAY: the page shell's `.sc` (the ROAS scorecards it was cloned from) takes flex-basis:170px under 820px, so on a phone a one-line lever note stood 170px tall in its tile — the card's captions are `.lvc` now. Half-typed form values survive a re-render a save elsewhere triggers. Harness tools/test_stocklevers.mjs (109), tools/check_stocklevers.js (60 — the matrix checks fail on the page before), tools/check_stockeven.js (43 — its negative control now forces the in-stock tiles 3 + 1; the band it used to force is gone). Docs: docs/RULES.md §3m. ONE RULE PER LEVER — CONNECTED IN ONE MARKET, READ IN EVERY MARKET (Ray, 6 Oct 2026: "the same rules (same rule name) will be applied/ copied across all different markets. Each individual stock lever will be connected to one rule, and that rule could be spotted or aggregated across different markets. For example, with Superdry UK and range completion, there are currently two overlapping logics rule on the dashboard; there should be only one that makes sense at any time. Allow a connection between a lever and a rule in a specific market, and then that same rule can be spotted or monitored across the remaining markets, to ensure monitoring between 30 markets is accurate"): with no rule connected a lever reads every rule that looks like it, and where MORE THAN ONE does in a market the matrix cell is DASHED, its tooltip counts them and the plan tile says where they overlap. ⛓ Connect a rule (plan tile) opens a picker: a market (the first read, or where it was last picked), the rules it runs that read as the lever — THE NAME THE MOST MARKETS CARRY FIRST (the copied rule), then run order — each with the markets carrying the same name ("in 2 of 2"), and "Every stock rule in GB" for the rest (marked "does not read as this lever"); Connect saves the plan lever `rule: {n, mk}` (the worker checks the name ≤160 and that mk is the brand's). Then EVERY market reads that rule ALONE, found BY NAME as FeedHero copies it (engine nameKey: case, spacing, typographic quotes/dashes set aside, every word kept — "< 0.21" ≠ "< 0.2"): found → that rule's reading (range completion still measured line first, then the connected rule's own stated cut-off; units its cut-off; hero on, or paused when the rule pauses), the other rules that would read as it set aside and NAMED in the tooltip, an idle rule said; MISSING → the cell reads "missing" in red, never another rule's value in its place, its tooltip naming what the market runs instead, off a set plan with "the connected rule … is not in this market" (a units lever missing its rule reads N/A, on plan when the plan is N/A). The column head names the rule and found/total (red when missing anywhere), the plan tile "in N of M markets · missing in …", the switch list names only the connected rule where it runs and keeps a market without it, flagged "⚠ … is not in DE — copy it there first" (the brief too), ⧉ Copy summary names each lever's rule or "no rule connected"; Change re-picks, ✕ disconnects. Harness tools/test_stocklevers.mjs (131) + tools/check_stocklevers.js (76 — its own page: overlap dashed, picker, connect, a GB-only rule missing in DE, copy-it-there-first, disconnect). Docs: docs/RULES.md §3n. THE SAME RULE UNDER A DRIFTED NAME — FOUND BY ITS JOB (Ray, 6 Oct 2026, pointing at Superdry FR's "Range Completion based Availability": "i see this rule in … (FR) market - but system is not picking it up"): the copies drift in NAME while their JOB never moves — read live that day, Superdry's hold rule is "Range Completion by Availability" in GB/ES/BE-NL, "Range Completion based Availability" in FR/DE/DK/FI and nine more, "Availability by Range completion" in IE, "Range Completion (BAU & Peak) - 20%" in IT/NL, every one writing Availability (NO and PL carry ONE name on two rules, one per field). A market's copy is now (engine findRule): the same name on the same field; else the rule DOING THE SAME JOB — reading as the lever and writing the same field, the closest name first (nameSim, joining words like "by"/"based" set aside; a rule picked from every stock rule finds its copy on the same field under a near name); else missing. Connect stores the field (`rule.d`, kept by the worker only as a database name); a lever connected before has it read off the market it was picked in (resolved, never written back). The cell's tooltip says "run here under another name", the plan tile and summary count them ("1 under another name", names in the tooltip), the picker counts where each rule would be found, the switch list names each market's own copy. On the live names: the hold rule connected in GB is found in all 19 markets (3 by name, 16 under another name), the RC-flag rule connected in FR in all 19 with each market's stated cut-off (21% / 20%). Harness tools/test_stocklevers.mjs (138 — the live name shapes, NO's one-name-two-fields, the closest of two, every-stock-rule copies, the legacy field read) + tools/check_stocklevers.js (82 — DE's "Stock < 11 -> OOS" found as GB's "Stock < 9 -> OOS", and a stub variant where DE truly lacks the rule: missing, copy it there first). FOUR LEVERS FOR EVERY BRAND, MARKETS THAT READ THE SAME IN ONE ROW, AND EVERY CARD FOLDS (Ray, 7 Oct 2026: "i thin the system on stock is still not optimised for view yet (especilly multi markets client) - every client should have these stock levers: 1. range completion 2. hero sizes 3. stock quantity threshold 4. stock based exclusion"; then "these button doesnt apply/ make sense" over the in-stock card's header switches, "this is good actually ! should be pushed to top" over the coverage matrix, and "each module info on Stock Management is collapsible pls - too many at once"): LEVERS = rc · hero · thresh · excl in his order (engine LEVERS ↔ worker LEVER_KINDS held equal), each reading ONE coverage-matrix mechanism; a brand with NO plan still reads all four off its rules (planLevers; All brands lists every brand in scope, "N of 4 planned" / "no plan yet", the first edit writes the plan); the stored `units` lever IS the stock-based exclusion (LEGACY units → excl, read everywhere, saved under excl by the worker — no KV migration); a threshold = a rule that HOLDS stock back (rules.js heldBack kind, else holdKind) — a calculation (Schuh's "Calculate stock count details"), a restatement ("Not available to Zero", every Superdry market) or a release is SET ASIDE and named in the tooltip; a name's "≤ 3" and a plan's "< 4" are one setting (compared as the units a product needs to stay live, mlOf/planMl), the cell printing the rule's own words; a scoped exclusion (Everest) keeps its range's rules from the threshold (sibbed); a lever nobody plans is no change and no blocker in a switch, and "Keep as it runs" leaves it out unless the market runs it. MULTI-MARKET: a brand with 8+ markets opens GROUPED — markets whose every cell is drawn the same, held to the same targets from the same source, in the same mode share one row (Superdry 19 → 8 mixes), each market a chip opening its own values under the group, biggest mix first; By market · Grouped per device (fcc-stock-lvgrp). The coverage matrix is FIRST on the page; the scenario/period switches left the in-stock card's header for the forecast panel (the column head names the forecast shown). EVERY CARD FOLDS: its title one button (chevron · title · a line that survives the fold), a fresh device opening the overview and the levers only, the device keeping what a person folds or opens by hand (fcc-stock-fold), a link/#hash/coverage-cell jump opening what it points at for the visit, ⊕ Expand all / ⊖ Collapse all; the phone's skim view leaves these headings to the page. Harness tools/test_stocklevers.mjs (162), tools/check_stocklevers.js (103, a nine-market stub build({ many: true })), tools/check_stockfold.js (Playwright, presync, 29). Docs: docs/RULES.md §3o
GET  /catalog (+GET /api/catalog/roster, /api/catalog/master, /api/catalog/master/file, /api/catalog/master/row, /catalog/engine.js) → CATALOGUE — every product, master → feed (Ray, 28 Sep 2026: "a module to browse / view / track / deep dive product catalogs … Google Merchant Center or Shopify interface … product ID first: image, title, price, sale price, availability … almost looks like an Excel format, but nicer … Include ROAS with a blue bar chart similar to new arrivals … when you hover over an image or product, you should have access to what we call a master feed source in FeedHero report MCP … the product data before FeedSpark optimization … a smooth transition of (before and after) between pre-optimization, update population, data optimization, and data enrichment … which field existed vs. didn't, which field is structured vs. didn't, which field is enriched vs didn't"). THREE SOURCES, ONE PRODUCT: the OUTPUT feed via /api/feed/proxy (Feed Lab parser, in the browser), the client's MASTER as FeedHero last imported it (the `master_feed` MCP on the SAME FeedHero_reports server + ROAS_MCP_TOKEN as /roas: /api/catalog/master = one page_size-1 call → file, headings, rows, last import, KV `catmaster:<cmpid>` 20 min, a first read that outlasts the MCP's 25 s = 202 'preparing' and the page retries; /api/catalog/master/file streams the import file untouched — zip / XML / CSV / TSV, unzipped + sniffed + parsed in the browser, strict UTF-8 falling back to Windows-1252 at the first bad byte (Schuh)), and Google Ads via /api/roas + /api/roas/live (its AGGS now also carry Product_age, Price_type, Colour, Age_group, the three optimisation statuses, GPC and custom labels 0–4). The company id is read off the WIRED output URL (/output_feeds/<cc>/<cmpid>/…, a -fb feed = its Google market's), never the query; the file is fetched only from https *.feedhero.net/import_feeds/<cmpid>/ (catFileOk), FeedHero's backup URLs never reach the browser, and it is served as BYTES (application/octet-stream, nosniff, attachment) — never FeedHero's content-type on our origin; every route scoped like /api/roas; NOTHING IN GIT (KV caches only; the harness fails on a committed master or a FeedHero import URL). THE JOIN IS READ OFF THE DATA: detectJoin tries every master column against g:id AND c:fs_data_original_id on a sample and keeps the one carrying the most ids (a Shopify master's parent product_id loses to its row id), no carrying column = no join, never a guess; last duplicate wins; master rows the feed never sends = the 'Not in feed' tab. Real Accessorize GB: 4,221/4,221 joined, 1,283 master-only, ~3 s. Known gap: source_url is the NEWEST import, so an id FeedHero dropped after the output build reads 'No master row', never a false 'populated'. LINEAGE (docs/catalog_engine.js, UMD FeedCatalog, served at /catalog/engine.js): 42 attributes in nine groups, each ONE status — kept · structured (format / remap / lift — material out of `composition`, gender out of the title or tags, the attribute's own column read first) · populated · optimised (rewrite / restructure / reprice / overlay, the overlay's source decoded off the URL) · enriched (new, or a list added to: '+2' images) · dropped (a sale price equal to the price says so) · missing — on four stages Master → Populated → Optimised → Enriched, plus the three questions Ray asked per field: ex (existed), spec (the value sent is in Google's format: GTIN check digit, '76.00 GBP', the availability vocabulary, a GPC path, title length, no HTML), en (FeedSpark added it). PRICE IS A MODEL, not a column (base = price; a NOW column below it is the sale; a WAS above it makes the base the sale; a was-only column BELOW the base is ignored — American Golf's mrrp); text compares without whitespace/entities/tags; the main image is never a 'more images' entry and one promoted to the main slot has moved, not been lost; md5 product_type slots are not keywords; apparel is read off the category, never a size. Completeness master → feed weighted like the Golden Record, on the one audit legend. WHICH GOOGLE ADS SEGMENT A PRODUCT SITS IN (measured 28 Sep 2026 on six clients, 1,992 SKUs over 19 category-filtered reads): Category = g:product_type slot 1 on its FULL PATH ONLY — FeedHero files a re-typed product under its OLD path until the next read (Superdry's 'Men > Swimwear' sits under 'Menswear'), so a nearest-ancestor row would claim it for a segment it is not counted in; such a product reads 'not in the ROAS tree yet' (a dashed box); brand/colour/gender/age group/GPC = the raw value case-insensitively (GPC as a path, duplicate names merged, 'Â£' repaired); price type = sale below price; PRICE BAND (Ray, 29 Sep 2026: "add price band to this Roas option list") = FeedHero's Price_group cut, placed on a price range read off each band's label and on whichever price (list vs what the shopper pays) the data says FeedHero bands on, a band named in words = totals only — NOT SET UP in FeedHero for any roster brand yet ("Reports not found for Price group" on all six with a ROAS report, 29 Sep 2026), so the card and the inspector say so and it fills in on its own once it is; title/keyword status = fs_data_opti T/Keywords; PRODUCT AGE = the item GROUP's earliest first-seen date, ≤7 d Brand new, ≤30 d New, then calendar QUARTERS (This season / Last season / This year = the two before / Perennial), counted at the ROAS read (99.45% placed vs 97.34% on the variant's own date); a cut FeedHero reads as empty (every row 'Unsorted' — Monsoon's custom labels) is NAMED and never placed; data-field review + batch are segment totals only; 'Unlisted SKUs in Ads traffic' belongs to no product; Accessorize has no ROAS report. THE PAGE (docs/FeedSpark_Catalog.html, on /design/fcc.css): hero (brand, Google/Meta, market chips, three source pills with live progress) · KPI band · New arrivals + ROAS by <any cut> (blue = ROAS's alone, click a bar to list its products) + What FeedSpark did · the VIRTUALISED table (ID, image, title pinned; price, sale, availability, a lineage strip per attribute, completeness, FeedSpark stamps, ROAS; ⊞ 20 more columns per device; S/M/L; Table/Grid; tabs All/In stock/On sale/New/Changed by FeedSpark/Needs attention/Not in feed; the search grammar field:value, a comma list ORs, - negates, quotes; ⬇ CSV with lineage codes, ⧉ IDs, → Brief) · the INSPECTOR (hover = a 380 ms dwell to open, 150 ms to follow, REAL pointer movement only — a row scrolled under a still cursor never moves it, the /stock lesson; docked beside the table, a bottom sheet on a phone; before/after image slider, the four-stage scrubber with ▶ Replay, completeness/fields/AI-ready, every field a row with a four-node rail → click for the word diff, the master column it came from, its status and Google's format; FeedSpark's stamps; the Google Ads segments it sits in — labelled the segment's, never the product's; the raw master row; ↑↓ P Esc) · Field lineage (every attribute × every product; click a segment to list those products). THE DASHBOARD IS TWELVE MODULES (Ray, 29 Sep 2026: "make it modularised / evenly spaced also, and generate 4-5 more interesting views"): one card size on a 3-column grid driven by a CONTAINER query on main (so the docked inspector re-flows it 3→2→1, never the window width), the KPI band one row of equal tiles each with its mini bar, ⊞ Modules hides/reorders per device (fcc-cat-mods, never shared state), and every bar is a table filter (facets spec/price/run/mix/depth via passModFacet): Data FeedSpark added (master→feed fill dumbbell per attribute), Google format issues (per-product spec warnings recorded while tracing, S.spw), Price bands (what the shopper pays, full vs on sale stacked, bands off the 5th–95th percentile on a round ladder), Size-run health (item groups 2+ variants by share in stock), Catalogue mix (brand/category/colour/gender/age/custom labels with on-sale share), Content depth (images/highlights/title/description/keyword slots vs Google's ranges); What FeedSpark did names each stage's top three attributes; a GPC/category ROAS cut names its leaf. STOCK CONTROL, THREE MORE MODULES → TWELVE, four even rows (Ray, 29 Sep 2026: "add stock control in the catalog modules as well, pull from stock management module"): ONE read of the SAME GET /api/rules/stock?brand= /stock draws (loadStock beside loadRoas), the market matched on the wired feed's FeedHero cmpid, never a name — Availability (the feed's own in/pre/back/out word, each bar a table filter, facet avail), Stock controls = /STOCK'S COVERAGE MATRIX FOR THIS MARKET (Ray, 29 Sep 2026, sending /stock's 'Which stock controls each market runs' table: "stock control module - i wanna see this instead"; an interim master → feed read, #559, was reverted by the same ask): one line per stock mechanism in /stock's own column order (availability · thresholds · range completion · labels · exclusions · local inventory · lifecycle — the market's single row turned on its side to fit the card), the rule count IN a cell shaded in /stock's one hue (darker = more rules, so colour is never the only identity), '—' where the market runs none, a Total, the 1-rule/N-rules legend, ☐ Split by channel (per device fcc-cat-stkch) adding each mechanism's channel counts, the rule names in the row tooltip and a click opening /stock?brand=&market=&mech=<k>; 'Stock ↗' opens the market, Ad spend kept off (/stock's forecast for this market: its LARGEST blocking rule — a market counts once, 'at least' — × spend per product a day × Conservative 5% / Aggressive 10% / the 100% ceiling, a month; no Google Ads read or nothing held back says so, never a guessed figure). The Northwind stub pushes rules_stub's synthetic rule list through the real rules engine. GOOGLE ADS, THIS PRODUCT'S OWN — LAST 7 DAYS (Ray, 29 Sep 2026: "catalog module - bring in adwords data 7days in these columns option too" → "isn't it in Ads Traffic from feedhero reports? (you can then match by product ID)"): FeedHero's reports server gained `ads_traffic` the same day (one row per advertised product as Google Ads returns it, keyed on the client's own Product ID; the lower-cased Ads item id stands in when none matched), so ⊞ Columns gains a 'Google Ads · this product · 7 days' group — Impr., Clicks, CTR, Cost, Avg. CPC, Conv., Conv. value, ROAS, Conv. rate, Cost / conv., OFF by default, a turned-on column being what reads the market; every rate off the product's own sums and BLANK (never 0%) with nothing under it; sortable; in the CSV. The join is the product id, case-blind (checked on Schuh UK and Superdry GB live feeds). Only products with an impression are listed, so once a read is WHOLE a missing product had none ('—' says so); the Google Ads pill counts served products and served products no longer in the feed; the inspector opens on the product's own 3×3 line above its segments. GET /api/catalog/ads (worker catAdsRead): a big account is 100+ pages of 200 (Schuh UK 22,283 rows, Superdry GB 26,673, Reiss GB 24,541), read 24 pages a call 4 at a time, progress kept in KV catads:<cmpid>:7_days, the page calling again until whole — only the whole read carries rows {id:[impr,clicks,cost,conv,value]}; sorted by the Ads item id; a report FeedHero re-downloaded mid-read (new window or row count) STARTS AGAIN rather than splicing two; whole read kept 12 h; 'still being prepared' = 202 and the page retries; 'No Ads Traffic data found' (Accessorize UK) = an answer kept a day; Meta refused; company off the wired feed; nothing in git. THE COMMERCIAL VIEW — SIX MORE MODULES → EIGHTEEN, six even rows (Ray, 29 Sep 2026: "create more modules for Catalog Dashboard - that can be viewed for let's say a brand procurement head not too close with FeedSpark day-to-day but understanding thing from a commercial perspective, choosing between different feed vendor"): read off THIS feed, never a claim — Optimised vs not (FeedHero's Google Ads cut by FeedSpark's title/keyword stamp: share of products vs revenue, REVENUE PER PRODUCT, ROAS, CTR, conversion; footer 'side by side, not a controlled test'), Before and after FeedSpark (master → feed completeness on the Golden Record weighting, products given a Google-required field their master lacked, values added / rewritten, AI-shopping six carried, what is STILL missing), Where the ad budget goes (30-day spend by top-level category bucketed by its OWN return: losing money <100% ROAS / below market / at or above), What the service does (capabilities with their evidence in this feed; unreadable = ·, never a zero), The service in numbers (products, feeds run for the brand, values per product, FeedHero rules, new products, values maintained), Fee check (the buyer TYPES a monthly fee + another vendor's: per product, per value, vs Ads revenue and spend, the lift that pays for it, the difference a year — empty until typed, kept on the device fcc-cat-fee, never sent). 👔 Procurement view = a preset of the same per-device module state (v:'proc': the six first + Ad spend kept off, Data FeedSpark added, ROAS; the rest put away; a second click restores all), /catalog?view=procurement opens it. The comparison table is .vs because .cmp was already the completeness bar (a flex row). Docs: docs/CATALOG.md › The commercial view. SIZE RUNS ARE THE MASTER'S (Ray, 30 Sep 2026: "i think this size-run health module is not accurate"): a feed that drops out-of-stock sizes (Superdry GB: 25,695 of 25,695 in stock) read every group as 'all in stock' by construction — group 278158 read 3 of 3 where the master holds 7 sizes, 3 in stock; every master row now joins its item group's run (E.runAdd/stockOf: NOT_AVAILABLE = out, quantity when there is no word, neither = no guess), used when half the feed's groups are found there, one count (grpC) for the card, its filter and the Variants column, the footer naming the out-of-stock sizes left out of the feed, and a feed with no master that is all in stock SAYS it cannot show a broken run. COLUMN FILTERS (Ray, 30 Sep 2026: "in catalog - allow filtering by column values as well"): a ▾ on every filterable header — values with counts (ticked = kept, find + Select all/Clear on what is shown), a number range for numeric columns, text (contains / doesn't / has a value / blank) for ID, title, GTIN, MPN, item group; a column's list is counted from what the OTHER filters leave (the spreadsheet rule); each filter a chip with ✕ beside the search, named on the CSV/brief line, reset with the market; the pop-up is viewport-fixed and follows its ▾ on scroll. COMPLETENESS IS GOOGLE-READY AS SENT (Ray, 30 Sep 2026: "i think these scoreding of masterfeed is too generous across the board"): a field scored in FULL the moment the master held it in any form (NOT_AVAILABLE, a price with no currency, "womens", HTML in a description) — FeedSpark's restructuring credited to the master; E.credit now scores Google's format = full, present-but-not = HALF, absent/buried in another column = 0, the SAME rule on the feed and in the inspector's stage scrubber (Superdry GB real files: master 76 → 63, feed 95 → 95); the format check now accepts http as well as https for links and images (Google takes either); the Before-and-after card gains 'Required values put into Google's format'. IMAGE PIXELS — WHAT EACH MAIN IMAGE SHOWS (Ray, 6 Oct 2026: "on Catalog - scan and crawl the pixel for client's imagery as well"): a nineteenth module, full-width under the eighteen, with 🔍 Scan (100–3,000 products of the table as filtered, unread first, fcc-cat-pixn). Each file is fetched through GET /api/catalog/img (same origin, so a canvas can read it — NO OPEN PROXY: behind scope + the wired-feed check, only a host the feed's own image_link / additional_image_link / overlay img_url uses, learned from the feed head into KV imghost:<c>:<m> 24 h with ONE fresh head read an hour before a 403, plus FeedSpark's overlay hosts; image only, never SVG, ≤20 MB, nosniff, private cache), drawn at ≤256 px and read by docs/pixel_engine.js (UMD FeedPixels at /catalog/pixels.js — arithmetic on the pixels, no model): size/format/bytes off the file, BACKGROUND off the frame's outer strip (plain = a pale backdrop on 40%+ of it or one colour on 60%+; a studio wall-and-floor sweep, a knee crop or a chair arm is still plain — the first cut, needing one uniform colour, called 19 of 21 Reiss pictures scenes), product FILL against each row's own edges, Laplacian SHARPNESS, a 256-bit dHash + the centre colour (the 64-bit cut merged four different Reiss garments on one studio wall). checks() keeps Google's stated requirements (min 100 / 250 apparel, 64 MP, 16 MB, format = fail) apart from its best practice (colour backdrop, scene, fill under 75–90%) and FeedSpark's own thresholds (<800 px, soft = bottom 5% AND under a quarter of the scan's median, ≥20 images), plus the same picture on 3+ item groups. GOOGLE'S 500 × 500 FROM 31 JAN 2027 (answer 6324350: "new image size requirements of at least 500 x 500 pixels for all products beginning January 31, 2027"): until that date an image under it is a Google WARNING naming the date (check small27, its own 'Under 500 px — Google from 31 Jan 2027' bucket); from it, a fail for every product, apparel or not (REQ.min2027/from2027; checks take o.now, pinned in tools/test_catalog.mjs). Calibrated on real Schuh / Reiss / Monsoon / Superdry images (nothing committed; Superdry's every main image is 658×779). Readings kept by IMAGE URL in KV catimg:<c>:<m> (PUT/GET /api/catalog/imgscan, catImgClean checks the post whole, newest 6,000); works on sheet-backed feeds. The module = eight buckets each a table filter (facet pix) + a To-look-at gallery (Google fails first, a tile opens the inspector); ⊞ Columns gains Image / Background / Product fill / Sharpness / Image issues; the inspector reads the picture out with 🔍 Read this image. Docs: docs/CATALOG.md › Image pixels. Harness tools/test_catalog.mjs (154: the engine on synthetic fixtures shaped like the real feeds, the real Feed Lab parser, an in-process zip, the segment rules, catMasterInfo/catFileOk LIFTED against a stub MCP + KV, route scope + headers, registry, nav, no client data) in qa_gate/presync/validate; tools/check_catalog.js (Playwright, presync, 36: drives the real page on the synthetic Northwind set of tools/catalog_stub.js — nine equal modules in three even rows, a price band and a size run filtering to an independent count, ⊞ Modules hiding/remembering/resetting, the join, a real hover, the dock, the pointer guard incl. Gecko's same-coordinate mousemove, stages + diff, exact placement, the style's age, facets/search/matrix against an independent count, the phone sheet; 4 fail with the guard removed and an ancestor fallback put back); the stub also feeds check_mobile / check_darkmode behind a guard on the page's file name. Docs: docs/CATALOG.md
GET  /aivis (+GET|PUT /api/aivis, PUT|DELETE /api/aivis/run, POST /api/aivis/ask, /aivis/engine.js) → AI VISIBILITY, LIVE (Ray, 29 Sep 2026: "lets build 2 AI Surface visibility tracker real-time" — idea #2 of the five competitive modules). Does a client's brand, site and products turn up when a shopper asks an AI answer engine the questions its catalogue answers? FIVE SURFACES, each switched on by its OWN Worker secret and listed "not connected" until then — ChatGPT (OpenAI Responses API + web_search, OPENAI_API_KEY; its search location FALLS BACK TO THE US unless sent, so the market's is always sent), Google AI Mode + AI Overviews (SerpApi google_ai_mode / google → ai_overview with the ~1-minute page_token follow-up, SERPAPI_KEY — Google's own page, the closest to a shopper), Perplexity (the Agent API /v1/agent — Sonar chat completions ended 27 Sep 2026, PERPLEXITY_API_KEY), Claude (web_search_20260209 called directly, streamed, effort low, ANTHROPIC_API_KEY); models overridable with AIVIS_CLAUDE_MODEL / AIVIS_OPENAI_MODEL / AIVIS_PPLX_MODEL, no redeploy. The ASKING is src/aivis.js (one question × one surface, normalised to {text, cites, results, searches, usage}; a Claude pause_turn is sent back unchanged) and POST /api/aivis/ask STREAMS it as NDJSON (start → search → sources → text → done, 10s heartbeat); the READING is docs/aivis_engine.js in the browser, so a stored run is re-read with a new competitor list: named + position among the tracked brands (a common-word brand only capitalised), own site cited vs only read (a domain whose label IS the brand is own with no setup), whose site every cited domain is (own/competitor/retailer/publisher/forum/review/reference/other — never guessed), products (⚡ Match streams the feed: a cited product page by path, a product named by its title core), untracked names the answers recommend (＋ Track). THREE HONESTY RULES: a BRANDED question never counts toward visibility or share of voice; a surface that showed NO answer (no AI Overview) or failed is out of every rate, counted apart; cost only where billing is published (Claude at list price, others none; a run over 30 answers asks first). Questions come from the brand's own PT tree (PT Guard snapshot — every third asks where to buy, two branded close the set; en + de templates), editable and team-shared in Setup. The page: KPIs (visibility · share of voice · own site cited · position · answers · Claude cost), the question × surface grid filling live (4 in flight, 2 per surface; a cell is updated in place so a click lands mid-stream; the drawer shows the answer AS IT IS WRITTEN), share of voice, visibility by surface, who the answers cite, also named, products, over time (a small chart per surface, one axis each) + run history, Setup, how it is measured; saved as answers land (throttled 5s, serialized) and final at the end, ⏹ Stop keeps what was answered and never saves a cut-off question as a failure. KV only: aiviscfg:<client> + aivisrun:<client>:<id> with the run's headline in the KEY'S METADATA (the history lists with no value reads), 400-day TTL; scoped per signin; asking/saving need the `aivis` grant (it spends money); -fb markets refused. Grantable module `aivis`. Harness tools/test_aivis.mjs (engine, every adapter vs stub payloads, the /api/aivis route lifted against a stub KV; qa_gate/presync/validate) + tools/check_aivis.js (Playwright, presync: grid + KPIs vs an independent count, drawer, a live streamed run, stop, track, match, 390px); tools/aivis_stub.js feeds check_mobile/check_darkmode a synthetic book. Docs: docs/AIVIS.md
GET  /restock (+GET /api/restock/roster, /api/restock/ads, GET|PUT /api/restock/ledger, /restock/engine.js) → RESTOCK — demanded in Google Ads, not buyable (Ray, 7 Oct 2026: "feedhero-reports have got By Products report - and 'Restock products' is interesting to build as a module"). FeedHero's reports MCP exposes no By-Products / Restock tool (roas_dashboard rejects the aggregation; the site is login-gated), so the module is built from the two reads it does expose, under a definition the page states: a RESTOCK PRODUCT is one shoppers are still asking for in Google Ads — an impression, a click, a conversion in FeedHero's trailing 30-DAY Ads Traffic read (the Catalogue's per-product lane, catAdsRead now taking a PERIOD: one record per company × period, catads:<cmpid>:30_days, a chunk a call, never spliced with the 7-day one; CAT_ADS_PERIODS allow-list twinned by RESTOCK.RESTOCK_PERIODS) — that CANNOT BE BOUGHT: the output feed sends it OUT OF STOCK, or no longer carries it at all (FeedHero's own "Unlisted SKUs in Ads traffic"). THE JOIN RUNS IN THE PAGE (docs/restock_engine.js, UMD FeedRestock, served at /restock/engine.js): the feed streams through /api/feed/proxy + the Feed Lab parser, columns read by name (id, title, link, image, availability, price, the FIRST product_type slot), the key is the product id CASE-BLIND (Google lower-cases the Ads item id that stands in for a blank one), four states never blurred — buyable (in stock / pre-order / backorder) · out of stock in feed · not in feed · AVAILABILITY NOT STATED (no word, or one the vocabulary cannot read — its own bucket, never read either way); ranked by conversion value, then conversions, clicks, impressions. THREE RULES because it goes in front of a client: nothing is joined against a feed that did not stream WHOLE (every product past a break would read "not in the feed"); MONEY NEVER CROSSES A CURRENCY (a mixed read sums impressions, clicks and conversions only, and the status line, KPI and split say why); DAYS UNAVAILABLE COME FROM A LEDGER, never from the window — FeedHero reports 30 days of demand, not when the stock ran out, so src/restock.js keeps KV restock:<cmpid> = [first seen unavailable, last seen, o|g, came back at] per product, written by the page's observation after each join (PUT {oos, gone, back, n, feedN}; cleanObs: an id is one thing, out of stock beats gone beats back; an observation with no lists is REFUSED so a failed feed is never written as "everything came back"), an episode kept while the product stays unavailable, a return stamped and kept RESTOCK_BACK_KEEP_DAYS (14) for the Back-in-stock view, a return-then-out-again a NEW episode, unseen 45 days → dropped, capped 6,000 oldest-seen first, TTL 120 days; tracking begins the first time a market is opened and the page SAYS SO ("Tracking since … · N readings", a product never recorded reads "first seen today") — never back-filled. Company id off the WIRED output feed (catCmpid), never the query; scoped like /api/catalog; a -fb market refused; a sheet-backed feed says FeedHero holds no report. Page docs/FeedSpark_Restock.html on /design/fcc.css (the Stock shell): brand select (the hours badge follows it) + market chips (a sheet market dashed, 'no Ads') · ⚡ Re-read Google Ads · ⬇ CSV · ⧉ Copy IDs · → Brief (Workflow, cat technical, 'Restock - demanded, not buyable - Brand MKT - MMYY' / 'Re-activate ads - back in stock …') · ↗ FeedHero (the Ads Traffic report on FeedHero's own site, the MCP's web_url shape) · six KPIs (served · not buyable · out of stock in feed · not in the feed · conversion value on them + spend still landing · back in stock) · the buyable / not split · where the lost demand sits (top-level category, a gone product under 'No longer in the feed', the tail folded) · the table (Restock · Back in stock · All demanded; sortable, filterable, 100 then Show all) · how it is read. Deep link ?brand=&market=&view=. Grantable module `restock`; wave 3 on the migration board. Harness tools/test_restock.mjs (qa_gate/presync/validate — the engine, the ledger, catAdsRead LIFTED and driven by period, routes, registries, no client data) + tools/check_restock.js (Playwright, presync — the real page on the synthetic Northwind set of tools/restock_stub.js: KPIs against an independent count, the three views, sort, filter, the observation PUT, the phone). Docs: docs/RESTOCK.md. OPEN: FeedHero's own Restock-products definition / columns (a screenshot aligns the page); moving the observation onto the xml-scan agent so days accrue without anyone opening the page.
GET  /migration (+GET|PUT /api/transform; /transformation 301s here — Ray, 28 Sep 2026: "change to /migration instead, makes more sense"; module slug stays `transformation` so existing grants hold, and a status recorded under `mod:/transformation` / `chk:m./transformation|` still reads) → AI TRANSFORMATION ROADMAP (Ray, 24 Sep 2026: "a month-by-month migration roadmap until completion … a roadmap for a live dashboard migration so I can keep track with my management and senior team … an internal roadmap to get all account managers to use the dashboard … 70% fixed … 20–30% customization"): the live tracker for moving the FCC off Ray's personal GitHub/Cloudflare onto FeedSpark-owned infrastructure (recommended path C, REBASED 24 Sep 2026 to Ray's timeline: ownership transfer in Nov → module-by-module migration onto a FeedSpark database COMPLETE BY DECEMBER 2026 (peak-week freeze 20 Nov–2 Dec, holiday freeze from 21 Dec) → January 2027 = the first live iteration with every AM → Jan–Feb iterations + AM-focused customisation → March measure/promote/compute decision; board full-screen toggle ⛶) AND the AM rollout (5 AMs in two waves, the 70/30 operating model, AM-built /x/ modules as config over scoped data sources). ROADMAP = git (the page, between RM:START/RM:END); STATUS = KV `transform` via mapStoreRoute (kvmerge per key: i:<item> d:<decision> k:<kpi> r:<risk> a:<am> p:<role>, each stamped by/at). HISTORY & ARCHIVE (Ray, 28 Sep 2026: "a pop-up panel on the right-hand side for any historic or archive actions — delete, remove, edit — of everything, because it will be co-worked by Matt and Andy and me"): the worker diffs the map before/after every merge and logs each moved key to KV `transformlog` (src/txhistory.js, cap 1000, Access identity — never the page's own stamp), GET /api/transform?history=1 behind the same opt-in gate; the 🕘 panel (hero + board toolbar, above full screen, below the editor) reads it in words with field diffs, filters by person/kind, ↩ undoes any entry (asks if changed again since), Archive tab = everything deleted now, ↺ restorable. PLAN REVIEWED 28 Sep against each module as it stands: wave 1 = Feed Lab/Deck generator/Pricer/Scheduled work, wave 2 = the whole feed-scan family (Label, PT, Golden, Volume, Overlays, Images — one scan lane), wave 3 = Task Manager/ROAS/AI Quote/Keyword calendar/Leadership/Activity, wave 4 = Workflow/Command center/this tracker; each module = the eight common steps + its own lines (TX.modLines, appended so earlier ticks keep their index); new cards for browser-only data, scan agents reading the feed map from the app, the news lane off Ray's Claude account. Harness tools/test_transform.mjs in qa_gate/presync/validate · EDITABLE BOARD + MODULE-BY-MODULE BADGES (Ray, 24 Sep 2026: "make the roadmap completely editable and maybe in a [Miro] format … my management team to actually work on this and keep an eye on the checklist, item by item. GET  /website                   → FEEDSPARK.COM REDESIGN MOCKUP (Ray, 30 Sep 2026: "devise a proper website … highlight the main benefit of having a dedicated feed management system in the age of AI … use some visuals of the system … host the mockup site on FCC too", then "replace Tachyon with SparkAI"). docs/FeedSpark_Website.html, served as a DOCUMENT (DOC_PATHS, like /case): the live editor for copy and no FCC chrome, since it must look like the public site; linked from the Leadership hub. v2 — EDITORIAL, AND THE PICTURES ARE THE REAL SOFTWARE (Ray, 1 Oct 2026: "website still looking a bit too 'AI done' — shake it up and use actual footage of FCC to pull in amazing data segmentation & visualisation"): numbered sections with the label in a margin column, hairline rules, flat buttons, plain dated copy and a captioned Fig. N under every picture — the v1 marker highlights, glows, sparkle icons, chat-bubble mock, channel orbit and rows of identical cards are gone. The footage (docs/website/, ~1.1 MB: three H.264 loops with poster frames + five WebP stills) is bundled as Data modules (wrangler rule **/website/*) and served at /website/<file> by mediaResponse — HTTP Range (206 / 416), because Safari will not play an MP4 from a server that ignores it; videos are muted/inline, play only on screen, never under reduced motion, each with a pause control. Positioning: every competitor studied (Rithum, smec, Feedonomics) sells feeds as one part of something bigger, so the page argues specialist-in-the-AI-era — what changed this year (dated, sourced), specialist vs commerce platform / media agency / in-house, the software, SparkAI with its guardrails and a before/after product, results by sector until the client signs off, Structure-Surge-Scale + the AI-readiness scale, services, audit, FAQ. A review layer (Review notes pins + Design notes drawer) lists what Ray confirms before launch (incl. showing the Command Center publicly at all); strip it for the live build.
